### [1] [Selecting Interpretable Circular Coordinates from Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08230)
*Vincent P. Grande,Marina Meila*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出CIRCOL方法，通过用户提供的字典从持续上同调检测到的循环中选择可解释的圆值坐标，将问题转化为向量拟阵中的最小权重基问题，并在离散点云上通过构造与L²内积一致估计的余链内积矩阵来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续上同调可以检测数据中的环状结构，但检测到的圆坐标通常是抽象的，无法直接告诉我们它对应哪个可测量的角度、相位、扭转或解码器。需要一种方法将这些抽象拓扑特征与科学上有意义的候选解释联系起来。

Method: 在连续设定中，每个候选坐标通过其拉回角形式的同调类表示，选择一组最小能量候选张成相关H¹子空间转化为向量拟阵的最小权重基问题。对于离散点云，提出CIRCOL方法，构造余链内积作为L²内积在非均匀采样下的一致估计量，利用投影矩阵选择低能量字典基并诊断拓扑平凡类或未解释的持久类。

Result: 在合成示例、分子模拟和头方向细胞的神经记录上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: CIRCOL能够从用户提供的科学意义候选字典中选择可解释的圆值坐标，桥接了抽象持续上同调结果与具体科学解释之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Circular coordinates obtained from persistent cohomology reveal loop structure in data, but they usually remain abstract: A detected circle does not tell us which measured angle, phase, torsion, or decoder explains it. We propose a method for selecting interpretable circle-valued coordinates from a user-supplied dictionary of scientifically meaningful candidates explaining the detected cohomology. In the continuous setting, each candidate is represented by the cohomology class of its pulled-back angular form, and selecting a minimum-energy set of candidates spanning the relevant $H^1$ subspace becomes a minimum-weight basis problem in a vector matroid. We then introduce CIRCOL, a method for discrete point clouds sampled from the manifold. We prove that the introduced cochain inner product is a consistent estimator of the $L^2$ inner product of fixed smooth 1-forms under non-uniform sampling. The resulting projection matrix both helps selecting a basis of low-energy dictionary coordinates and diagnoses topologically trivial candidates or unexplained persistent classes. Finally, we verify the effectiveness of our method on synthetic examples, on molecular simulations, and neural recordings of head-direction cells.

</details>


### [2] [Homology of the Lie Algebra of Locally Generated Derivations of a Discrete and Proper Metric Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08455)
*Nigel Higson,Tsuyoshi Kato*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文为每个真离散度量空间$X$关联一个李代数，该李代数通过局部生成的导子作用在由$X$的点索引的矩阵代数的无限张量积上。当$X$是$n$维欧氏空间中的整数格点时，计算了该李代数在平凡标量系数下的同调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究离散度量空间与李代数之间的关联，特别是通过无限张量积上的导子作用来构造李代数，并理解其同调性质，以探索几何与代数之间的深层联系。

Method: 为每个真离散度量空间$X$构造一个关联的李代数，该李代数由作用在矩阵代数的无限张量积上的局部生成导子构成。针对$X$为$n$维欧氏空间中的整数格点这一特例，计算该李代数在平凡标量系数下的同调群。

Result: 成功计算了当$X$是$n$维欧氏空间中的整数格点时，该李代数在平凡标量系数下的同调。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了离散度量空间与李代数同调之间的桥梁，并为更一般度量空间的类似计算提供了基础。

Abstract: We associate to each proper discrete metric space $X$ a Lie algebra that acts by locally generated derivations on an infinite tensor product of matrix algebras indexed by the points of $X$. We compute the homology of this Lie algebra with trivial scalar coefficients when $X$ is the integer lattice in $n$-dimensional Euclidean space.

</details>


### [3] [High-degree cohomology of congruence subgroups of $\text{SL}_n(\mathcal{O})$ via cohomology of $S$-arithmetic groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08600)
*Matthew Scalamandre*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了数环𝒪的素理想𝔭对应的主同余子群的上同调空间中Steinberg表示的重数问题，证明了最高次上同调中Steinberg表示的重数为1，并在𝒪为欧几里得环且𝔭为通用边除子等条件下证明了次高次上同调中该重数为0。证明基于Blasius–Franke–Grunewald关于S-算术群上同调在某一线性度范围之外的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解主同余子群的上同调作为SL_n(𝒪/𝔭)表示的结构，特别是Steinberg表示在其中出现的重数，这是表示论与数论交叉领域的基础性问题。

Method: 利用Blasius–Franke–Grunewald的工作，通过计算S-算术群在某一线性度范围之外的上同调来推导重数结论。

Result: 最高次上同调中Steinberg表示的重数为1；在𝒪为欧几里得环且𝔭为通用边除子等条件下，次高次上同调中Steinberg表示的重数为0。

Conclusion: 主同余子群的上同调空间作为SL_n(𝒪/𝔭)表示时，Steinberg表示仅在最高次上同调中以重数1出现，在次高次上同调中在特定条件下不出现。

Abstract: If $\mathfrak{p}$ is a prime ideal of a number ring $\mathcal{O}$, then the top-degree cohomology of the principal congruence subgroup of level $\mathfrak{p}$ is naturally a representation of $\text{SL}_n(\mathcal{O}/\mathfrak{p}).$ We prove that the multiplicity of the Steinberg representation in this cohomology space is one. When $\mathcal{O}$ is Euclidean and $\mathfrak{p}$ is suitably small -- for example a universal side divisor -- then we prove that the multiplicity of the Steinberg representation in the next-highest-degree cohomology space is zero. Our proof relies on a computation of the cohomology of an $S$-arithmetic group ouside of a linear range of degrees, derived from work of Blasius--Franke--Grunewald.

</details>

### [4] [Thermodynamic Structure and Composition in Nonlinear Convection-Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07829)
*J. J. Segura*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向开放非线性对流-扩散系统的连续介质优先框架，通过自由能平衡、非负体耗散及明确的边界与源贡献来形式化热力学一致性。该框架将非线性输运系统定义为由可容许状态场、存储泛函、本构通量分解、源项和边界端口构成的结构化对象。证明了热力学平衡在精确结构保持变换、子域限制、局部到全局重构以及开放子系统的功率互连下保持不变。进一步推导了经典线性对流-扩散模型作为平衡态切向热力学后代，并证明在弱形式、半离散化和全离散时间步进中相同不变量得以保持。以非线性漂移-扩散和多孔介质对流-扩散为实例，最终构建了一个在连续建模、子系统耦合、线性近似和计算中第二定律始终可见的组合输运框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性对流-扩散系统中，模型是否能在子域限制、界面耦合、近平衡线性化和计算离散化等运输分析自然操作下保持一致的热力学平衡，是关键问题。现有方法缺乏一个既能处理开放系统又能保证热力学一致性贯穿建模、耦合、近似和计算全链条的统一框架。

Method: 采用连续介质优先方法，将热力学一致性形式化为自由能平衡（含非负体耗散和显式边界/源贡献）。将系统定义为结构化对象（状态场、存储泛函、通量分解、源、边界端口），证明该结构在四种操作（结构保持变换、子域限制、局部到全局重构、功率互连）下不变。通过平衡态切向线性化导出经典线性模型，并在弱形式、半离散和全离散数值格式中验证热力学不变量的保持。

Result: 建立了热力学一致性在精确变换、子域限制、局部-全局重构和子系统互连下的保持性定理；推导出经典线性对流-扩散模型作为平衡态切向热力学后代；展示了非线性漂移-扩散和多孔介质对流-扩散两个实例；证明了数值离散（弱形式、半离散、全离散）中热力学结构的保持条件。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一个组合输运框架，使得热力学第二定律在连续建模、子系统耦合、线性近似和数值计算的全过程中始终保持可见。该框架为开放非线性对流-扩散系统提供了从物理建模到数值计算的端到端热力学一致性保障。

Abstract: Nonlinear convection--diffusion systems play a central role in transport phenomena, including mass transfer, heat transfer, porous-media transport, and coupled continuum processes with source, exchange, and interface effects. In such systems, the key question is often not only which governing partial differential equation is used, but whether the model preserves a consistent thermodynamic balance under the operations that arise naturally in transport analysis: restriction to subdomains, coupling across interfaces, linearization near equilibrium, and discretization for computation.
  This paper develops a continuum-first framework for open nonlinear convection--diffusion systems in which thermodynamic consistency is formulated as a free-energy balance with nonnegative bulk dissipation and explicit boundary and source contributions. Within this setting, nonlinear transport systems are defined as structured objects built from admissible state fields, storage functionals, constitutive flux decompositions, sources, and boundary ports. We prove that the thermodynamic balance is preserved under exact structure-preserving transformations, restriction to subdomains, local-to-global reconstruction over compatible domain decompositions, and power-conserving interconnection of open subsystems. We then derive classical linear convection--diffusion models as tangent thermodynamic descendants at equilibrium and show that the same invariant survives weak formulation, semidiscretization, and fully discrete time stepping when the numerical design respects thermodynamic structure. Nonlinear drift--diffusion and porous-medium convection--diffusion are used as explicit examples. The resulting contribution is a compositional transport framework in which the second law remains visible across continuum modeling, subsystem coupling, linear approximation, and computation.

</details>


### [5] [Admissible Discrete Linear Propagators for High-Order Time Splittings of Rotational Nonlinear Schrödinger Equations with Arbitrary Three-Dimensional Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07923)
*Tianqi Zhang,Fei Xue*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对含拉普拉斯算子和三维旋转算子的非线性薛定谔方程，研究了鲁棒的高阶时间分裂方法。发现连续精确分解在离散后不保证方法自伴性，识别了局部对数中的二次偶项导致精度阶依赖于初始数据。提出了两种可容许的离散线性传播子（对称显式精确积分器和回文广义剪切传播子），它们满足酉性、一阶一致性、方法自伴性和奇数局部对数，数值实验验证了缺陷机制并恢复了设计的二阶、四阶和六阶精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高阶时间分裂方法在离散后因旋转算子导致线性传播子失去方法自伴性，使实际收敛阶依赖于初始数据，需要构造具有良好代数结构的传播子以恢复设计精度。

Method: 通过傅里叶伪谱离散化，分析显式精确积分器的局部对数结构（识别二次偶项），提出固定网格可容许性条件，构造对称显式精确积分器和回文广义剪切传播子两种可容许传播子。

Result: 识别了局部对数二次偶项导致的状态依赖精度缺陷；两种可容许传播子满足酉性、方法自伴性、一阶一致性和奇数局部对数；数值实验验证了缺陷机制并恢复了二阶、四阶、六阶收敛行为。

Conclusion: 通过构造具有适当代数结构（奇数局部对数、方法自伴）的离散线性传播子，可消除状态依赖的精度退化，实现鲁棒的高阶时间分裂方法。

Abstract: We study robust high-order time splittings for nonlinear Schrödinger equations whose linear part is defined by the Laplacian and an arbitrary three-dimensional rotation operator. After Fourier pseudospectral discretization, a continuous exact factorization of the linear flow need not yield a method self-adjoint fixed-grid propagator. For the original stage-wise explicit exact integrator, we identify a quadratic even term in the local logarithm and show that its visibility is state-dependent, so the observed temporal order of accuracy can depend on the initial data. We then formulate fixed-grid admissibility for discrete linear propagators and construct two admissible propagators for arbitrary three-dimensional rotation: a symmetrized explicit exact integrator and a palindromic generalized shear propagator. Both are unitary, first-order consistent, method self-adjoint, and have odd local logarithms. Numerical experiments verify the predicted defect mechanism and demonstrate recovery of the designed second-, fourth-, and sixth-order behavior with the admissible propagators.

</details>


### [6] [Off-site enforcement of natural conditions on smooth boundaries for finite elements upon fitted straight-edged triangular meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07963)
*Vitoriano Ruas*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅使用直边网格、通过修正双线性形式来恢复曲界逼近精度的有限元方法，用于解决反应扩散方程中自然边界条件的不准确施加问题，并给出了严格的可靠性分析和数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对光滑域边界上Neumann或Robin边界条件因曲域被直边三角形/四面体网格逼近而导致精度损失的问题，传统方案（如Barrett和Elliott, 1988）采用曲边单元拟合真实边界，但本文希望探索一种仅使用直边单元、不依赖几何拟合的替代途径。

Method: 采用仅由直边单元构成的拟合网格，通过在双线性形式中添加额外项来补偿因多边形逼近曲域所丢失的精度，这些额外项模拟了在逼近边界上施加的、更接近真实边界条件的自然边界条件。该方法应用于三角形Lagrange有限元，用于求解反应扩散方程。

Result: 给出了该方法的严格可靠性（rigorous reliability）分析，并通过数值实验支持了理论结果。

Conclusion: 仅使用直边网格并修正双线性形式，可以有效恢复因曲域多边形逼近而损失的边界条件精度，为反应扩散方程的有限元求解提供了一种替代曲边单元的有效方案。

Abstract: A few decades ago some possible remedies to an inaccurate enforcement of Neumann or Robin conditions prescribed on the boundary of a smooth domain, owing to the approximation of a curved domain by the union of straight-edged triangles or tetrahedra in a fitted mesh, were addressed in the literature. By that time authors such as Barrett and Elliott (1988) advocated the use of elements with a single curved edge or face fitting the true boundary not only at two or three vertexes, but also at additional points on those curves or curved surfaces, so as to define a polynomial surface of a certain type compatible with the theoretical approximation order of the method in use. In this work we adopt a different approach, whose main feature is the use of a fitted mesh consisting of straight-edged elements only. The recovery of lost accuracy due to the domain's approximation by a polytope is achieved by means of the addition of terms to the bilinear form, which account for natural boundary conditions of the same type to be prescribed on the approximating boundary, though much closer to the true ones. This technique is applied here to the case of triangular Lagrange finite elements, for which we give a rigorous reliability study in the solution of reaction-diffusion equations. Numerical experimentation is supplied in support of the theoretical results.

</details>

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对弹性波逆介质问题，提出了一种新颖的时域直接采样方法，利用单次入射源和时移相关策略实现散射体的可靠成像与识别，无需传统的P-S波分解，并通过理论分析和数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弹性波逆介质问题中，传统方法通常需要对入射源的时间剖面施加特定假设，且依赖P-S波分解，限制了方法的适用性和效率。因此，本文旨在提出一种无需上述假设和分解的时域直接采样方法。

Method: 提出时域直接采样方法，引入时移相关策略替代传统的P-S波分解，利用走时对齐机制从耦合弹性波场直接成像；通过Parseval恒等式将成像泛函变换到频域，并利用修正贝塞尔函数分析其渐近行为。

Result: 成像泛函在目标位置处达到最大值，从而能够可靠地识别散射体；数值实验验证了方法的性能与应用可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的时域直接采样方法为弹性波逆介质问题提供了一种无需先验假设和波场分解的高效成像手段，兼具严格理论保障和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This work is concerned with an inverse medium problem for elastic waves, in which unknown inhomogeneities are reconstructed from time-resolved boundary measurements. We propose a novel time-domain direct sampling method for locating scatterers from a single incident source, without imposing specific assumptions on the temporal profile of the excitation. In particular, the imaging functional introduces a time-shifted correlation strategy that replaces the traditional $P$-$S$ wave decomposition with a travel-time alignment mechanism, thereby enabling direct imaging from the coupled elastic wave field. To analyze the proposed time-domain imaging functional, we employ Parseval's identity for the Fourier--Laplace transform and reformulate the functional in the frequency domain. By exploiting properties of modified Bessel functions, we characterize the asymptotic behavior of the imaging functional and show that it attains its maximum at the target location, which enables reliable identification of the scatterer. Rigorous theoretical justifications are provided to substantiate the effectiveness of the proposed method. Numerical experiments are also presented to demonstrate its performance and applicability.

</details>


### [8] [Multi-type Sensor Placement for PDE-based Bayesian Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08074)
*Steven Maio,Alen Alexanderian,Karina Koval,Ruanui Nicholson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对偏微分方程约束的贝叶斯反问题，提出了一种多类型传感器（不同精度和观测类型）的最优实验设计框架。通过将问题形式化为背包约束的二进制优化问题以最大化期望信息增益，并提出了具有理论保证的随机成本-效益贪心算法。对于非线性反问题，利用贝叶斯近似误差框架构建误差校正全局线性模型，证明了近似EIG是精确EIG的下界。通过两个PDE模型反问题验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传感器最优布局方法多针对单一类型传感器，缺乏同时考虑多种精度和观测类型传感器的统一框架，且对于非线性反问题缺乏可扩展的近似求解策略。

Method: 1. 将OED问题形式化为背包约束的二进制优化问题；2. 提出随机成本-效益贪心算法（对线性高斯问题具有单调子模集函数理论保证）；3. 对非线性问题，采用贝叶斯近似误差框架构建误差校正全局线性模型，以近似EIG作为替代目标。

Result: 对于线性高斯反问题，所提贪心算法具有理论保证；对于非线性反问题，近似EIG是精确EIG的下界，提供了有原则的替代目标。两个PDE模型反问题的数值实验验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架为多类型传感器在贝叶斯反问题中的最优布局提供了统一且可扩展的解决方案，兼具理论保证和实际可行性。

Abstract: We address optimal placement of multi-type sensors for Bayesian inverse problems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs). The proposed framework allows for sensors with different accuracies and observation types. We formulate the optimal experimental design (OED) problem as a knapsack-constrained binary optimization problem for maximizing expected information gain (EIG). To approximately solve the resulting optimization problems, we propose a stochastic cost-benefit greedy algorithm, which admits theoretical guarantees for monotone submodular set functions. Specifically, these guarantees apply in the case of linear Gaussian inverse problems with uncorrelated measurement errors, where the EIG admits a convenient closed-form expression. For nonlinear inverse problems, we develop a non-intrusive approach that uses the Bayesian approximation error framework to define an observation model with an error-corrected global linear model. We show that the corresponding approximate EIG is a lower bound for the exact EIG and thus provides a principled surrogate objective for the OED problem. The effectiveness of the proposed methods is demonstrated in two model inverse problems governed by PDEs.

</details>


### [9] [Algorithm XXXX: Computation of finite element degree-of-freedom transformation matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08172)
*Matthew W. Scroggs,Garth N. Wells*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用自动化算法，用于任意Ciarlet型有限元自由度映射的构建，无需针对每种元素单独实现，已在FEniCSx的Basix库中实现，支持运行时用户自定义元素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶有限元方法在现代CPU和GPU架构上具有更高的算术强度，能更充分利用计算能力，但缺乏通用的、自动化的算法来高效构建任意阶数元素的自由度映射，现有方法需要为每种元素单独实现。

Abstract: The arithmetic intensity of algorithms for computing finite element operators increases with increasing polynomial degree. This has made high degree methods particularly attractive on modern CPU and GPU architectures, since on these architectures performance at low degree is limited (severely) by the available memory bandwidth and only a very small fraction of the floating point capacity of the processor is used. Higher degree methods can exploit a significantly greater fraction of the available compute power of modern architectures. However, whilst stable methods for computing high-degree finite element bases are well-established, there is no universal and automated algorithm for the efficient construction of the degree-of-freedom map for arbitrary degree elements. We address this with a new algorithm that can be used in computing degree-of-freedom maps for an arbitrary Ciarlet-type finite element using only the element's definition and properties of the reference cell, and without requiring a specific implementation for each element. This method is implemented in the library Basix, a component of the FEniCSx libraries. As well as allowing vast simplifications of parts of a codebase, the algorithm allows for new elements to be implemented with ease and has allowed us to support user-defined custom elements that a user can create at runtime without requiring the user to input any information about transformations required to construct a degree-of-freedom map.

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and analyze a Continuous Interior Penalty (CIP) stabilized Virtual Element Method (VEM) for three-dimensional advection-diffusion-reaction equations on general polyhedral meshes. Abstract: We develop a complete flux finite volume method for convection-diffusion equations that works on arbitrary meshes in two and three dimensions and for discrete spaces of any polynomial degree. Unlike standard finite volume discretizations, where the numerical flux is directly approximated from the flux definition, we derive the exact normal flux across each control volume edge/face from the underlying PDE. This exact flux splits naturally into a homogeneous part (the classical Scharfetter-Gummel flux) and an inhomogeneous part expressed via a Green's function that incorporates the tangential flux and the source term. The resulting formulation is exactly equivalent to the continuous equation and, once the discrete space is chosen, yields high-order schemes without any correction or stabilization.
  For piecewise linear spaces, the scheme achieves optimal second-order accuracy in convection-dominated regimes and can preserve positivity on moderately coarse meshes. For quadratic spaces, standard finite volume methods, based on the Lagrange elements or B-splines, fail to attain optimal $L^2$ convergence unless the control volume mesh is specially designed. The proposed complete flux scheme, however, always achieves optimal $L^2$ convergence independently of the control volume mesh. Numerical experiments in two and three dimensions confirm the robustness and optimal accuracy of the approach.

</details>

Abstract: A method is presented to carry out a transient simulation of eddy current problems with nonlinear materials. Coils are voltage-driven. The magnetic field due to currents in coils are considered by their Biot-Savart-fields. The magnetic vector potential is used in the finite element formulation. The time stepping method is based on implicit Euler. The arising nonlinear equation system is split into two parts, the common finite element system and a circuit equation. Each part is solved separately by Newton's method. Additionally, a line search is used to solve the nonlinear field equations. Inrush currents and average magnetic flux densities through cross sections of laminates of a nonlinear benchmark problem consisting of a laminated iron core inserted in a cylindrical coil are studied. All details of the numerical benchmark are given to evaluate the presented results. Numerical data describing the performance of the presented method are provided.

Abstract: We prove expression rate bounds of finite-parametric, spectral and neural surrogates for holomorphic maps between separable Hilbert spaces. We work under an algebraic decay assumption on the eigenvalues of the covariance of the Gaussian measure on the input space. We obtain convergence rates for mean-square errors, and additionally in first-order Gaussian Sobolev spaces, to account for errors in the approximation of gradients.

Abstract: In this paper, we study the approximation of gradient flows of harmonic maps, which serve as model problems for applications in micromagnetics, liquid crystals, and nonlinear plate bending. Harmonic maps are vector fields that are critical points of the Dirichlet energy subject to the constraint that the vector field be unit length pointwise. Most existing time-stepping schemes for gradient flows deal with the constraint by linearizing the unit length constraint at every step, which involves solving for the solution increment in the tangent space of the constraint. These schemes lead to robust control over the violation of the constraint, but require solving degenerate saddle point systems at every step that may be difficult to precondition. In this paper, we propose a scheme that first computes the unconstrained increment and then projects this increment pointwise onto the tangent space. With an additional stabilization, this scheme is energy stable under mild step size restrictions and provides robust control of the unit length constraint violation. Our new scheme only requires the solution of decoupled symmetric positive definite systems at every step, which translates to a large increase in computational efficiency. We also propose a computable a posteriori criterion and a variable time-stepping procedure that guarantee the stability of the scheme. We conclude with computational examples demonstrating the efficacy of the scheme, and present a computational extension of the scheme to nonlinear plate bending.

</details>

TL;DR: 本研究汇编了2020-2025年间65所关闭或合并的美国高校数据，结合贝叶斯模型、降维、聚类和主题建模等方法，分析了 closures 的地理分布、机构共同特征及媒体报道模式。研究发现 closures 集中在东北部和中西部，关闭机构具有异质性（财务结构、区域人口统计和办学使命各异），宗教背景在媒体报道中反复出现。研究将结果定性为探索性和描述性，并讨论了其对即将到来的"招生断崖"的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COVID-19疫情导致的高校关闭和合并浪潮，可能为未来十年预期中的"招生断崖"（demographic enrollment cliff）提供一个早期但不完美的预演。作者希望通过系统分析这些 closures，为面临招生断崖的院校提供经验参考。

Method: 汇编65所关闭或合并高校的数据集，配对机构特征与州/区域级人口、经济和金融指标，并补充新闻语料。采用贝叶斯模型、降维（dimensionality reduction）、聚类（clustering）和主题建模（topic modeling）进行多维度分析。

Result: Closures 在东北部和中西部更为频繁（与人口预测一致），但绝对数量仍较小。关闭机构呈现异质性而非单一模式：财务结构、区域人口统计和办学使命是区分因素；宗教背景在媒体报道中反复出现。

Abstract: The COVID-19 pandemic produced a modest wave of college closures and mergers that may offer an early, if imperfect, preview of the demographic "enrollment cliff" anticipated in the coming decade. This paper examines the institutions that closed or merged between 2020 and the end of 2025. We assemble a dataset of 65 such institutions, pairing institutional characteristics with state- and regional-level demographic, economic, and financial indicators, and supplement it with a corpus of news coverage of the closures. Using a combination of Bayesian models, dimensionality reduction, clustering, and topic modeling, we describe where these closures occurred, what the closed institutions had in common, and how they were discussed publicly. Consistent with prior demographic projections, closures were more frequent in the Northeast and Midwest, though the absolute numbers remain small. The closed institutions were heterogeneous rather than uniform: financial structure, regional demographics, and institutional mission each contributed to distinguishing them, and religious affiliation recurred prominently in media coverage. We frame these results as exploratory and descriptive given the small sample, and we discuss what they may, and may not, imply for institutions navigating the enrollment cliff.

</details>

*Wen-Ting Wang*

TL;DR: 本文利用Uniswap协议费用开关的自然实验，通过匹配重叠事件研究双重差分设计，估计了流动性提供者（LP）对费率削减的供给响应（K_L），发现短期内活跃流动性和局部深度无显著变化，LP参与度和组成也无明显反应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定费率AMM（如Uniswap v3）无法分离费用收入、逆向选择损失（LVR）、路由响应和流动性供给等渠道，也无法评估反事实的动态费率规则。需利用真实的费率变化来识别LP供给响应。

Method: 采用预先指定的匹配重叠事件研究双重差分（Difference-in-Differences）设计，利用Uniswap协议费用开关造成的LP费率削减（分级差异化强度，但交易者端费率不变），从公共日志中重构处理组、事件时间、单元角色和结果变量，构建冻结的哈希校验面板数据。

Result: 未检测到活跃流动性或局部深度在短期内的平均显著响应；LP参与度和组成的更精确估计也未显示变化。名义费用收入不满足平行趋势假设，仅作描述性报告。Token-1交易量也未满足平行趋势条件。

Conclusion: LP侧响应K_L是设计驱动的可识别估计量；交易者端动态费率保护是模型条件下的边界，而非第二个可估计量。研究结果在设计分辨率下为未检测到效应，而非精确为零。

Abstract: Automated market maker (AMM) fee rules are often evaluated by liquidity-provider (LP) welfare, but that objective mixes fee revenue, adverse-selection loss (loss-versus-rebalancing, LVR), routing response, and liquidity supply. Fixed-fee Uniswap v3 history cannot separate these channels or identify counterfactual trader-facing dynamic-fee rules. Real fee-related variation nonetheless exists: the Uniswap protocol-fee switch cut LP take-rates with tier-differentiated intensity while leaving trader-facing fees unchanged. Using a pre-specified matched-overlap event-study difference-in-differences design, we estimate the liquidity-supply response to take-rate cuts, the kernel K_L that simulator-based fee-controller evaluations routinely freeze, while reconstructing treatment, event time, unit roles, and outcomes from public logs into a frozen, hash-checked panel before any estimate. We detect no large short-run average response in active liquidity or local depth; LP participation and composition, more precisely estimated, likewise show none, so the result is a non-detection at the design's resolution rather than a precise zero. Token-1 volume and native fee income fail the parallel-trends gate and are reported descriptively. A channel-admissibility audit delimits the estimand: the LP-side response K_L is design-based, while trader-facing dynamic-fee protection is a model-conditioned boundary, not a second estimand.

</details>


### [19] [Accounting for overdispersion and clustering in binomial data from N-of-1 trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08722)
*Majnu John,Heejung Bang,Stephanie Winkelbeiner,Philipp Homan*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文介绍了两种针对N-of-1试验（以二项变量为主要结局）的汇总分析策略：第一种考虑超二项变异，第二种额外考虑层次聚类。通过真实数据分析和模拟研究对两种方法进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N-of-1试验虽以个体患者结果为主要目标，但跨患者汇总结果同样具有临床和政策意义。现有方法在处理二项结局变量的汇总时，未充分解决过度离散和层次聚类问题。

Method: 提出了两种汇总分析策略：方法一考虑超二项变异（过度离散）；方法二在此基础上进一步考虑层次聚类结构。通过真实数据分析和模拟研究进行方法比较。

Result: 通过真实数据分析和模拟研究，展示了两种方法在考虑不同数据特征（过度离散和层次聚类）时的表现差异。

Conclusion: 两种策略为N-of-1试验中二项结局变量的跨患者汇总提供了可行方案，方法二在存在层次聚类时更具优势。

Abstract: N-of-1 trials are patient centered randomized controlled trials. Although the primary goal of N-of-1 trials is to obtain the results for each patient separately, pooling the results across patients also has relevance. In this paper, we present two analytical strategies to pool the results across N-of-1 trials, when the main outcome for each patient is a binomial variable. Our first method takes into account the extra-binomial variation, while as the second approach takes into account hierarchical clustering in addition to overdispersion. We illustrate the methods using real data analysis and compare the methods using simulations.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: Belle II 实验利用 408 fb⁻¹ 数据，通过 e⁺e⁻ → γa, a → γγ 过程寻找类轴子粒子（ALP），在 0.17–9.80 GeV/c² 质量范围内未发现显著信号，在 0.17–5.00 GeV/c² 范围内给出了目前最严格的 ALP-光子耦合上限，灵敏度达 10⁻⁴ GeV⁻¹，相比此前结果最高提升 9 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类轴子粒子（ALP）是标准模型诸多扩展方案中预言的粒子，通过与光子的耦合为可见与隐藏扇区之间提供了一个理论依据充分的门户。实验上寻找 ALP 有助于检验超出标准模型的新物理。

Method: 利用 Belle II 探测器在 SuperKEKB 对撞机上采集的 408 fb⁻¹ 数据，通过 e⁺e⁻ → γa, a → γγ 过程产生 ALP，选取三光子末态事件，在双光子不变质量谱中重建窄峰信号，扫描 0.17–9.80 GeV/c² 质量范围。

Result: 未观察到超过背景的显著信号，在 95% 置信水平下给出生产截面和 ALP-光子耦合 g_{aγγ} 的上限，灵敏度达 10⁻⁴ GeV⁻¹ 量级。在 0.17–5.00 GeV/c² 质量范围内给出目前最严格的限制，相比之前结果最高改善 9 倍。

Conclusion: Belle II 实验在 0.17–9.80 GeV/c² 范围未发现 ALP 信号，并在大部分质量区间（0.17–5.00 GeV/c²）提供了迄今最强的 ALP-光子耦合上限，显著推进了该类粒子的实验约束。

Abstract: Axion-like particles (ALPs) are predicted in many extensions of the Standard Model and provide a well-motivated portal between visible and hidden sectors through their coupling to photons. We search for ALPs produced in the process $e^{+}e^{-}\toγa$, $a\toγγ$, using a data sample corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $408~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ recorded by the Belle~II detector at the SuperKEKB $e^{+}e^{-}$ collider. Events containing three photons are used to reconstruct the ALP as a narrow peak in the di-photon invariant mass spectrum over the range $0.17 < m_{a} < 9.80~\mathrm{GeV}/c^{2}$. No significant excess above background is observed. We set 95\% confidence level upper limits on the production cross section and on the ALP-photon coupling $g_{aγγ}$, reaching sensitivities at the level of $10^{-4}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$. The limits are the most restrictive to date over nearly the entire mass range $0.17 < m_{a} < 5.00~\mathrm{GeV}/c^{2}$, and improve upon previous results by up to a factor 9.

</details>


### [21] [Early Career Issues in Particle Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08131)
*Saptaparna Bhattacharya*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文从早期职业研究者（ECR）的视角，探讨了粒子物理学在希格斯玻色子发现后所面临的未来规划、职业挑战与机遇，包括对撞机前景、就业市场、科学传播、人工智能以及支持网络等关键议题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子物理学正处于关键十字路口：2012年发现希格斯玻色子完成了标准模型，但随之而来的一系列基本问题（如层次问题、暗物质本质、中微子质量起源等）亟需新一代实验来解决。全球正在进行的长期规划将决定该领域直至2060年后的发展方向，而这些决策直接影响着负责未来设施设计、建造和数据分析的早期职业研究者（ECR）的职业生涯。

Method: 本文基于一次相关主题的演讲整理而成。首先从ECR视角调查了未来对撞机格局；其次通过大规模调查收集ECR的直接反馈，聚焦其核心关切和可行建议；然后对学术就业市场、科学传播的作用以及人工智能带来的双重挑战与机遇进行了分析。

Result: 研究呈现了ECR对未来对撞机格局的看法，通过大规模调查汇总了他们的主要关切和行动建议，揭示了学术就业市场的现状，评估了科学传播的未被充分认可的价值，以及人工智能作为双刃剑的影响。

Conclusion: 论文强调了ECR在粒子物理学未来发展中的关键作用，指出支持网络对于帮助他们应对复杂且不确定的职业道路至关重要。

Abstract: Particle physics stands at a crossroads. The discovery of the Higgs boson in 2012 completed the Standard Model, but it also opened a new set of fundamental questions about the nature of our universe. Answering these questions, from the hierarchy problem and the nature of dark matter to the origin of neutrino masses, will require a new generation of powerful and sophisticated experiments. The global community is currently engaged in a comprehensive, long-term planning process to determine the next big machine. These discussions are not merely technical. The decisions made in the coming years will shape the field until 2060 and beyond. This extended timeline has a direct and immediate impact on the lives and careers of the field's youngest members: the graduate students, postdoctoral fellows, and other early-career researchers (ECRs) who will be tasked with designing, building, and ultimately analyzing the data from these future facilities. This proceeding provides an overview of the key issues facing ECRs in particle physics today, based on a talk delivered on the subject. It begins by surveying the future collider landscape from an ECR perspective. It then delves into the critical feedback gathered directly from ECRs through large-scale surveys, focusing on their primary concerns and actionable recommendations. Following this, it presents an analysis of the academic job market, the important but often unrewarded role of science outreach, and the emerging dual-edged challenge and opportunity of artificial intelligence. The paper concludes by highlighting the indispensable support networks available to ECRs, which are vital for navigating this complex and uncertain career path.

</details>

### [22] [SASGeo: Stability-Aware Semantic Map Localization for GNSS-Denied UAVs -- A Framework and Synthetic Proof of Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07737)
*Natalia Trukhina,Vadim Vashkelis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAS语义地图定位框架，通过道路、建筑等持久性结构特征实现GNSS拒止环境下的无人机绝对定位。在220次随机检索试验中，空间语义匹配变体达到94.5-95.5%的Recall@1，优于全局语义描述符的58.6%，验证了结构化语义几何在跨视角扰动下的定位判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS拒止环境下无人机需要绝对位置修正来约束视觉-惯性里程计的漂移，但现有跨视角图像检索对季节、光照、视角、地图时效和传感器模态敏感，缺乏鲁棒的定位方法。

Method: 提出SAS语义地图定位框架，包含语义栅格对齐、关系图证据、特征稳定性与地理区分度、显式正/矛盾/未知观测，以及完整性感知的歧义修正拒绝机制。具体设计了加权和决策模型，支持全局语义描述符和空间语义匹配两种变体。

Result: 在220次随机试验中（含旋转、尺度变化、裁剪、遮挡、地图变化和语义干扰），全局语义描述符Recall@1为58.6%，空间语义匹配变体达94.5-95.5%。Wilson 95%置信区间显示全局描述符与空间变体间存在显著差异，但各空间变体间差异不显著。

Conclusion: 结构化语义几何在受控跨视角扰动下能有效区分位置，但尚未验证实际飞行导航性能。明确了后续需要解决的更难问题：别名混淆、地图老化以及修正拒绝测试。

Abstract: GNSS-denied unmanned aerial vehicles require occasional absolute position fixes to bound the drift of visual-inertial odometry. Cross-view image retrieval can provide such fixes, but raw appearance is sensitive to season, illumination, viewpoint, map age, and sensor modality. We propose \sas, a semantic map-localization framework that represents the environment through persistent structures such as roads, buildings, waterways, railways, intersections, and field boundaries. The method combines semantic raster alignment, relational graph evidence, feature stability and geographic distinctiveness, explicit positive/contradictory/unknown observations, and integrity-aware rejection of ambiguous fixes. Unlike a broad architecture-only proposal, this paper specifies concrete weighting and decision models and reports a reproducible synthetic proof of concept. In 220 randomized retrieval trials with rotation, scale changes, partial crops, occlusion, simulated map changes, and hard semantic decoys, a global semantic descriptor achieved 58.6\% Recall@1, while spatial semantic matching variants achieved 94.5-95.5%. Wilson 95\% intervals separate the global descriptor from the spatial variants but overlap among the spatial variants, so the experiment supports semantic geometry rather than a definitive benefit from each proposed module. The preliminary experiment does not validate real-flight navigation; rather, it demonstrates that structured semantic geometry can discriminate locations under controlled cross-view perturbations and identifies the harder aliasing, map-aging, and rejection tests required next.

</details>


### [23] [Physics-Guided Biomechanical Gait Adaptation for Humanoid Locomotion on Extreme Sloped Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07830)
*Xuanyu Chen,Mohan Liu,Dengchen Mei,Zhihao Gu,Haitian Zhang,Kaimin Mao,Haiyue Zhu,Shijun Yan,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HumoSlope的两阶段物理引导框架，用于实现机器人在多样斜坡地形上的鲁棒双足行走。第一阶段通过斜坡自适应ZMP正则化建立地形一致的平衡先验，第二阶段引入生物力学斜坡步态适配器(BSGA)动态调节步态以防止姿势退化。实验表明该方法可在无外部感知的情况下盲穿62.7%(32.1°)的户外草坡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无模型强化学习方法在平坦地形上实现了令人印象深刻的双足运动，但在陡峭斜坡上的控制问题尚未得到充分探索。与平坦或离散地形不同，斜坡地形施加持续的重力偏差，要求同时保持稳定性和姿态控制。在通用奖励公式下，策略容易收敛到缓慢、保守的低重心蹲伏步态。

Method: 提出两阶段物理引导框架HumoSlope。Stage I：建立地形一致的平衡先验，引入斜坡自适应ZMP正则化器，在局部倾斜支撑平面上评估而非世界水平参考面。Stage II：引入生物力学斜坡步态适配器(BSGA)，利用提取的宏观地形描述符作为特权训练信号，动态门控软奖励先验，根据估计的斜坡几何形状调节重心高度和下肢协调——鼓励髋部主导的上坡推进和膝部主导的下坡制动。部署的actor完全基于本体感觉，无需在线外部感知。

Result: 广泛的Sim-to-Real实验表明，该框架有效缓解了姿势退化，实现了盲穿户外草坡高达62.7%(32.1°)的连续行走。

Conclusion: 验证了一种物理引导方法在挑战性斜坡地形适应中的有效性，使双足机器人能够在无外部感知的情况下鲁棒地在陡峭斜坡上行走。

Abstract: Model-free reinforcement learning has enabled impressive humanoid locomotion; however, control on steep slopes remains largely unexplored. Unlike flat or discrete terrains, sloped terrains impose a persistent gravitational bias that demands simultaneous stability and posture control. Consequently, under generic reward formulations, policies can converge to slow, conservative low-center-of-mass (CoM) crouched gaits.
  In this work, we propose a novel two-stage physics-guided framework, dubbed HumoSlope, dedicated to robust humanoid locomotion on diverse sloped terrains. Specifically, Stage I establishes a terrain-consistent balance prior by introducing a slope-adaptive Zero Moment Point (ZMP) regularizer evaluated directly on the local inclined support plane rather than a world-horizontal reference. To prevent the resulting policy from defaulting to a crouched posture, Stage II introduces the Biomechanical Slope Gait Adapter (BSGA). Utilizing extracted macroscopic terrain descriptors as privileged, training-only signals, BSGA dynamically gates soft reward priors to modulate CoM height and lower-limb coordination based on the estimated slope geometry -- encouraging hip-dominant uphill propulsion and knee-oriented downhill braking. Crucially, the deployed actor remains entirely proprioceptive, requiring no online exteroceptive sensing.
  Extensive Sim-to-Real experiments demonstrate that our framework effectively mitigates posture degeneration and enables blind, continuous traversal of outdoor grass slopes up to 62.7% ($32.1^\circ$), validating a physics-guided approach to challenging slope terrain adaptation.

</details>


### [24] [Shift & Drift: A Zero-Shot Benchmark for Generalizable and Robust Autonomous Driving Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07844)
*Alessandro Canevaro,Hang Yu,Julian Schmidt,Peizheng Li,Silvan Lindner,Wilhelm Stork,Georg Martius,Julian Jordan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Shift & Drift双轨基准测试，分别评估运动规划器在语义分布偏移（跨城市、跨场景的零样本泛化）和状态分布偏移（执行误差下的鲁棒性）上的表现。实验发现，模仿学习方法在分布内表现优异但泛化和鲁棒性差，而强化学习方法具有更优雅的性能退化。揭示了模仿保真度与闭环韧性之间的经验性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环运动规划器在大规模数据集上训练后虽表现良好，但其对**新颖城市拓扑结构的泛化能力**以及**执行扰动后的恢复机制**尚未被充分探索。作者希望通过构建一个系统性基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建双轨基准：(1) **语义偏移轨**：通过转换流水线将DeepScenario Open 3D航拍数据集转化为nuPlan仿真框架，实现零样本评估；(2) **状态分布偏移轨**：向自车动力学注入随机扰动以量化对执行误差的鲁棒性。基于此，系统评估了不同规划范式（模仿学习与强化学习）的失效模式。

Result: 模仿学习方法在分布内基准测试中得分高，但在语义偏移下（尤其是行人密集场景）显著失败，且在有时间相关性的执行噪声下存在持续漂移。强化学习方法则表现出更优雅的性能退化，在两条轨上均保持更高的安全性和进度指标。

Conclusion: 揭示了**模仿保真度与闭环韧性之间的经验性权衡**，为社区提供了一个严谨的基准来评估运动规划器向可靠部署的进展。

Abstract: While closed-loop motion planners trained on large-scale, object-level datasets, e.g., nuPlan, demonstrate strong in-distribution (ID) performance, their generalization to novel urban topologies and recovery mechanisms following execution perturbations remain under-explored. To address this, we present Shift & Drift, a novel dual-track benchmark designed to rigorously stress-test motion planners across two critical axes of distribution shift: (1) The Semantic Shift Track leverages a novel conversion pipeline that transforms the aerial, DeepScenario Open 3D dataset into the nuPlan simulation framework. This enables zero-shot evaluation of planners trained on North American and Singaporean data against 1,182 scenarios spanning four German cities and the US city of San Francisco featuring dense pedestrian-cyclist interactions. (2) The State-Distribution Drift Track injects stochastic perturbations into the ego vehicle's dynamics to quantify robustness against compounding execution errors. Based on this, we systematically evaluate the failure modes of diverse planning paradigms under semantic and state-distribution shifts. While imitation learning methods achieve high scores in ID benchmarks, they exhibit significant failures under semantic shift, particularly in pedestrian-dense environments, and suffer from persistent drift when subjected to temporally correlated actuation noise. In contrast, the evaluated reinforcement-learning-based planner demonstrates more graceful degradation, maintaining higher safety and progress metrics across both tracks. Our findings reveal an empirical trade-off between imitation fidelity and closed-loop resilience, providing the community with a rigorous benchmark to evaluate progress toward reliable deployment.

</details>


### [25] [STEMbot: A Compliant Robot for Under-Canopy Plant Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07873)
*Zachary Charlick,Nilay Roy Choudhury,Haoyu Ma,Xiaonan Huang,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了STEMbot，一种专为植物冠层下自主导航设计的微型攀爬机器人系统。该系统集成了PIN-SLAM定位与建图、语义OcTree地图、流形约束A*路径规划器及射线追踪目标指定方法，实现了在植物茎干上的可靠攀爬与遮挡目标检测。实验表明，STEMbot能在7-33mm的茎干上自主导航，重建精度（Chamfer距离）小于1cm，为有机农业害虫早期检测提供了新方案。
